with over 25 years of industry experience and continues to        新。

The Group believes that, leveraging on its years of                本集團相信，憑著多年的行業經驗且在行業技術
industry experience and continual innovation in industrial         方面持續追求創新，本集團在爭取新項目時較現
technologies, the Group possesses a competitive advantage          有市場競爭對手以及新入行者更具競爭優勢，再
over existing market competitors as well as new entrants in        加上其作為可靠大氣污染防治解決方案供應商
new projects, and can also obtain more new projects from           日益提高的聲譽，本集團可獲取更多來自國家電
customers in the national electricity industries and also from     力企業以及其他行業客戶的新項目。
other industries due to its enhanced reputation as a reliable
atmospheric pollution control solution provider.

To achieve the binding targets of the “14th Five-Year Plan”        為實現「十四五規劃」及「十五五規劃」具約束力
and the anticipated objectives of the “15th Five-Year Plan,”       的目標，中國持續加強節能降碳政策。目前，年
China continues to strengthen its energy conservation and          綜合能耗5,000噸標準煤及以上的企業已全面納
carbon reduction policies. Currently, enterprises with an annual   入 節 能 審 查 範 圍，以 強 化 能 耗 與 碳 排 放 的 源 頭
comprehensive energy consumption of 5,000 tonnes of                管理。依據「十四五規劃」，至2025年，全國單位
standard coal equivalent and above are fully incorporated into     GDP能耗須比2020年下降13.5%，單位GDP二氧
the energy conservation review system, enhancing source            化碳排放須下降18%。官方數據顯示，當前進展
management of energy use and carbon emissions. According           符合規劃預期。
to the “14th Five-Year Plan,” by 2025, the country’s energy
consumption per unit of GDP must decrease by 13.5%, and
carbon dioxide emissions per unit of GDP must decrease
by 18% compared to 2020 levels. Official data indicates that
current progress is in line with the plan’s expectations.

Looking ahead to 2026, the nation will build upon the “14th        展 望 二 零 二 六 年，國 家 將 在「十 四 五 規 劃」基 礎
Five-Year Plan” foundation to promote more ambitious energy        上，推動更積極的節能降碳目標，預計進一步收
conservation and carbon reduction targets. It is expected that     緊重點行業能耗標準，並擴大碳排放管控範圍，
energy consumption standards for key industries will be further    為「十五五規劃」的開局實施奠定基礎。這將持續
tightened, and the scope of carbon emission controls will          驅動製造業加快綠色轉型，廣泛應用高效節能與
be expanded, laying the groundwork for the implementation          低碳技術。
of the “15th Five-Year Plan.” This will continue to drive the
manufacturing sector to accelerate its green transformation
and adopt high-efficiency, energy-saving, and low-carbon
technologies more widely.

Looking forward, more effort will be made by the Group to          展望未來，本集團將繼續積極提升研發實力並積
enhance its research and development capabilities and to           極 尋 找 合 適 的 收 購 項 目，從 而 擴 張 本 集 團 的 研
actively seek appropriate acquisition projects, to expand its      發、製造及銷售能力，以及進入新的國內和國際
capabilities of research and development, manufacturing and        市場。
sales, as well as to access new domestic and international

The Group believes that its established customer base in the       本集團相信，本集團多年來在中國建立的客戶基
PRC and its exposure to overseas markets could help it lay         礎及接觸海外市場的經驗，有助本集團奠下日後
a solid foundation for future expansion in both domestic and       在國內外市場擴充的穩固基礎，並使本集團成為
overseas markets of the Group, and enable it to become the         大氣污染防治解決方案行業的尖端。
leading player in the atmospheric pollution control solution
industry.

The Group will continue to seize market opportunities,             本集團將繼續把握巿場機遇，保護藍天碧水，打
protect the blue sky and clear water and build a century-          造百年天潔！
lasting Tengy!

Management Discussion and Analysis
管理層討論及分析


OVERVIEW                                                        概述

The Group is a first-class manufacturer of environmental        本集團是專業致力於環保產品設計、製造、安裝
protection products in China specializing in the design,        和服務的國內一流環保產品製造商，也是國內除
manufacture, installation and servicing of environmental        塵裝置和煙氣脫硫脫硝裝置等大氣污染治理的
protection products as well as a general contractor for air     工程總承包，主要專注於顆粒物的排放控制，在
pollution control projects such as precipitators and flue gas   多個行業為客戶提供特大型除塵器。本集團擁有
desulfurisation and denitrification devices in China, with a    多年的行業經驗且在行業技術方面持續追求創
primary focus on particulate emission control by offering       新。
mega-sized precipitators to customers in various industries.
The Group has years of industry experience and a record of
continual innovation in industrial technologies.

The Group has formed an all-round technology research and       本集團形成了技術方案設計、工程設計中心和技
development system comprising technical scheme design,          術 改 造 專 案 提 升 等 全 方 位 的 技 術 研 發 體 系。本
engineering design centers and technical transformation         集團在杭州設有研發中心，聘用10多名專業工程
project improvement. The Group has an R&D center                師，彼等的專業範疇分別在環境工程、土建工程
in Hangzhou and employs more than 10 professional               和機電工程。另外，在本集團的設計中心亦聘用
engineers, whose fields of expertise are environmental          多名專業人才。本集團成為紹興市級的研發中心
engineering, civil engineering and mechanical and electrical    和技術中心。
engineering. In addition, the Group’s design center also
employs a number of professionals. The Group has become
a municipal R&D center and technology center in Shaoxing.

During the Year, the Group generated its revenue primarily      本年度，本集團的收益主要產生自(i)銷售環保設
from (i) sales of environmental protection equipment; (ii)      備；(ii)銷售材料；及(iii)提供服務。
sales of materials; and (iii) rendering of services.

The Group’s sales of environmental protection equipment         本集團銷售環保設備指本集團為客戶提供的度
represented tailor-made and integrated atmospheric              身定製的綜合大氣污染防治解決方案，包括按項
pollution control solutions offered by the Group to its         目向客戶提供工程設計、設備採購及製造、指導
customers, comprising engineering design, equipment             安裝及調試、客戶培訓及維修與維護服務。
procurement and manufacturing, supervision of installation
and commissioning, customer training, and repair and
maintenance services provided to its customers on a project
basis.

During the Year, the Group mainly offered three types           本年度，本集團主要提供三種除塵器：靜電除塵
of precipitators: electrostatic precipitators, bag filter       器、袋式除塵器及減少二氧化硫及氮氧化物排放
precipitator and SO2 and NOx emission reduction                 （脫硫及脫硝裝置）
                                                                        。
(desulfurisation and denitrification devices).

The Group’s sales of materials represented sales of             本集團銷售材料指向關聯方或獨立第三方銷售
materials, including raw materials, spare parts and             的材料，包括原材料、備件和部件及廢料。
components and scrap materials to related parties or
independent third parties.




8     Zhejiang Tengy Environmental Technology Co., Ltd 浙江天潔環境科技股份有限公司
Management Discussion and Analysis
管理層討論及分析


The Group’s rendering of ser vices represented its               本集團提供服務指本集團按獨立基準向客戶提
technology consultancy services provided to its customers        供技術諮詢服務，包括向並非由本集團建造的項
on a st and-alone basis, which include repair and                目提供維修及更換，以及現場工程及維護服務。
replacement, and on-site engineering and maintenance
services for those projects which were not constructed by
the Group.

Precipitators are widely installed at coal-fired power           由於除塵器已在燃煤電廠、冶金廠、造紙廠及其
plants, metallurgical plants, paper mills and other industrial   他工業生產廠房廣泛安裝，因此，本集團的客戶
production plants. As such, the Group has an extensive           群極為廣泛，包括發電廠及工業生產廠房的項目
range of customers including the project owners of power         擁有人，或承包發電廠及工業生產廠房的建造工
plants and industrial production plants, or contractors          程的承包商。
who undertake the construction work of power plants and
industrial production plants.


BUSINESS REVIEW                                                  業務回顧

PROSPECTS                                                        未來展望

The super-large dust collector industry in China is positioned   在日益強化的監管要求、持續的工業現代化及技
for continued development opportunities through 2026             術進步的共同推動下，中國超大型除塵器行業在
and beyond, driven by the confluence of strengthening            二零二六年及未來將迎來持續的發展機遇。隨着
regulatory requirements, ongoing industrial modernization,       中國從「十四五」時期過渡到「十五五」時期，環保
and technological advancement. As China transitions from         政策框架預計將保持對減排的重視，同時可能針
the 14th Five-Year Plan period to the 15th Five-Year Plan, the   對特定污染物和工業流程引入更嚴格的標準。
policy framework for environmental protection is expected
to maintain its emphasis on emissions reduction while
potentially introducing more stringent standards for specific
pollutants and industrial processes.

The expansion of the national Emissions Trading System to        全國碳排放權交易市場擴圍至鋼鐵、水泥和鋁行
cover steel, cement, and aluminum sectors, with plans to         業，並計劃於二零二七年涵蓋所有主要工業排放
encompass all major industrial emitters by 2027, will create     源，將為先進環保設備創造持續的需求。隨着碳
sustained demand for advanced environmental protection           成本在運營經濟性中成為日益重要的因素，這些
equipment. Enterprises in these sectors will face increasing     行業的企業將面臨越來越大的經濟激勵，促使他
economic incentives to reduce their emissions through            們通過採用高效除塵系統來減少排放。本集團預
the adoption of high-efficiency dust collection systems,         期，這一監管演變將在未來幾年為設備銷售和服
as carbon costs become an increasingly significant factor        務提供帶來大量機遇。
in operational economics. The Group anticipates that this
regulatory evolution will generate substantial opportunities
for equipment sales and service provision in the coming
years.

protection devices to meet market demands for green              約運營成本的綠色技術的需求。我們將特別注重
technologies that deliver both environmental compliance          開發能耗更低、自動化能力更強、並能更好集成
and operational cost savings. Particular emphasis will be        數字化監控平台的除塵系統。本集團的產品組合
placed on developing dust collection systems with reduced        擴展將涵蓋互補的環保設備，包括灰渣處理系統
energy consumption, enhanced automation capabilities,            和煙氣脫硫脫硝裝置，從而通過提供綜合解決方
and improved integration with digital monitoring and             案來提升本集團的市場競爭力。
control platforms. The Group’s product portfolio expansion
will encompass complementary environmental protection
equipment including ash handling systems and flue

In 2026, the Group expects the implementation of the 80%         二零二六年，本集團預計，為實現二零二八年水
ultra-low emissions compliance requirement for cement            泥熟料產能80%超低排放合規要求，將推動水泥
clinker production capacity by 2028 to drive a wave of           行業迎來一波設備採購和設施升級潮。同樣地，
equipment procurement and facility upgrades across the           鋼鐵行業超低排放標準的持續執法，將為現有除
cement industry. Similarly, continued enforcement of ultra-      塵設備的更換和升級帶來持續需求。本集團憑藉
low emissions standards in the steel industry will generate      其成熟的產品能力和客戶關係，處於有利地位以
ongoing demand for replacement and upgrade of existing           把握這些市場機遇。
dust collection equipment. The Group is well-positioned
to capitalize on these market opportunities through its

2015. The Company recognises and embraces the benefits          策。本公司明白及信納董事會多元化能提升其表
of having a diverse Board to enhance the quality of its         現質素的裨益。甄選人選按一系列多元化範疇為
performance. Selection of candidates is based on a range        基準，包括但不限於性別、年齡、文化及教育背
of diversity perspectives, including but not limited to         景、種族、專業經驗、技能、知識及服務任期。最
gender, age, cultural and educational background, ethnicity,    終按人選的長處及預期可為董事會提供的貢獻
professional experience, skills, knowledge and length           而作決定。
of service. The ultimate decision is based on merit and
contribution that the selected candidates is expected to
bring to the Board.

of this policy, including reviewing the composition of the      進行至少一次檢討董事會成員的組合，當中考慮
Board at least annually by taking into account the benefits     所有相關多元化層面的裨益，以及在提出董事任

with this policy when making recommendations for Director
appointments.

   On 25 April 2015, the Company entered with TGL, the holding               For further details regarding the
   company of the Group, into a power supply agreement which was             TGL Power Supply Agreement,
   renewed on 1 January 2025, the renewed agreement ending on                please refer to the section headed
   31 December 2025 (“TGL Power Supply Agreement”), under                    “Connected Transactions” in the
   which TGL would procure supply of electricity to the Company in           Prospectus.
   the premises on lease from TGL in relation to daily operation of the
   Company’s office as well as the manufacturing and processing of
   goods which include the outer casings of the precipitators. Reason
   for entering into the subject agreement was that such premises
   are owned by TGL and accordingly the power supply services
   registered with the relevant power enterprises are in the name of
   TGL. In light of the foregoing circumstances, any procurement for
   alternate source of power supply would cause unnecessary costs
   and disruption. Whilst no additional costs would be charged by TGL,
   the amount payable by the Company under the TGL Power Supply
   Agreement for the provision of electricity supply was determined
   on with reference to the fee scale implemented by the PRC
   Government. Payment would be furnished to TGL on a cost basis
   on 25th day of each calendar month in accordance with the actual
   amount of electricity consumed by the Company of that particular
   month, and such consumption amount should be confirmed by both
   parties to the TGL Power Supply Agreement by jointly inspecting
   the readings on the relevant meters on 5th day of each calendar
   month. The amount of total utility payment to TGL for the Year is
   included in note 36 to the Financial Statements. The total utility fees
   payable to TGL in 2025 under the TGL Power Supply Agreement was
   approximately RMB2,023,956.

   於二零一五年四月二十五日，本公司與本集團控股公司TGL訂立供電                                           有關TGL供電協議的更多詳情，請
   協議，且於二零二�年一月一日續約，續約年期截至二零二�年十二                                            參閱招股章程「關連交易」一節。
   月三十一日止（ 「 TGL供電協議」）。據此，TGL將就向TGL租用的物業
   為本公司取得電力供應，以應本公司辦公室日常營運和製造及加工
   貨品（包括除塵器的外殼）之需。訂立上述協議的理由是該等物業由
   TGL擁有，故在相關電力企業登記的供電服務乃以TGL的名義進行。
   有見及此，若需取得任何其他供電來源，將產生不必要的成本及造成
   業務中斷。TGL不會收取額外成本，而本公司根據TGL供電協議就電
   力供應應付的金額乃參考中國政府執行的價格範圍釐定。本公司將
   根據每月實際耗電量於每個曆月第25天按成本向TGL支付電費，而
   耗電量應由TGL供電協議訂約雙方在每個曆月第5天共同讀取相關電
   表讀數後確認。本年度我們應向TGL支付的公用事業費用總額載於財

     Materials for a term of three years up to 31 December 2026.            dated 15 January 2024.
     Under the terms of this Master Supply Agreement, Tianjie New

     於二零二四年十二月十五日，本公司（作為承租人）與諸暨佳領（作為                                        詳情請參閱本公司日期為二零
     出租人代理）就租賃位於中國浙江省諸暨市環城東路 251 號的天潔                                       二五年四月十四日的公告
     大廈第23層（建築面積為1,176.11平方米）訂立第一份租賃協議，自二
     零二五年一月一日起至二零二五年十二月三十一日為期一年，每年
     租金為人民幣483,000元。




64   Zhejiang Tengy Environmental Technology Co., Ltd 浙江天潔環境科技股份有限公司
Directors’ Report
董事會報告


    Description of the transaction                                           Inquiry Index
    交易概述                                                                     查詢索引

    On 11 April 2025, the Company as lessee entered into the lease
    agreement with TGL as lessor for the leasing of the Plant and
    Equipment for a term of on year commencing from 14 April 2025
    and expiring on 13 April 2026. Reasons for entering the lease are
    that it would be beneficial for the Company to enter into the lease
    agreement and continue to lease the Plant and Equipment as it
    will enable the Company to secure its stable operation without
    incurring additional costs and expenses in identifying, renovating and
    relocating to other plant and ensure that there will be no disruption
    to the operations, business and growth of the Group. The annual cap
    for the said lease will not exceed RMB3.5 million.

    於二零二五年四月十一日，本公司（作為承租人）與TGL（作為出租人）
    就租賃廠房及設備訂立租賃協議，租期為一年，自二零二五年四月
    十四日起至二零二六年四月十三日屆滿。訂立該租賃之原因為，訂立
    租賃協議並繼續租賃廠房及設備對本公司有所裨益，此舉可讓本公
    司確保其穩定營運，而毋須因物色、翻新其他廠房並遷往該處而產生
    額外成本及開支，並確保本集團之營運、業務及發展不受干擾。所述
    租賃的年度上限將不超過人民幣3.5百萬元。

CLOSURE OF REGISTER OF MEMBERS                                   暫停辦理股份過戶登記

The registration of the transfer of Shares will be suspended     股份過戶登記手續將於二零二六年六月二十五日
from Thursday, 25 June 2026 to Tuesday, 30 June 2026             （星期四）至二零二六年六月三十日（星期二）止

     or loss and derivative instruments which are carried at            具修訂。
     their fair values/fair values less costs to sell.

     The preparation of consolidated financial statements               編製符合香港財務報告準則會計準則的綜
     in conformity with HKFRS Accounting Standards                      合財務報表須採用主要假設及估計，亦要
     requires the use of key assumptions and estimates. It              求管理層於應用該等會計政策的過程中作
     also requires management to exercise its judgments                 出判斷。該等涉及關鍵判斷的範疇及假設
     in the process of applying the accounting policies. The            及估計對該等綜合財務報表而言屬重大的

     assumptions and estimates are significant to these
     consolidated financial statements are disclosed in note
     4 to the consolidated financial statements.

     and has the ability to affect those returns through its            集團的現有權力賦予其目前掌控有關業務

     entity when the Group has existing rights that give it             則本集團對該實體擁有權力。
     the current ability to direct the relevant activities, i.e.
     activities that significantly affect the entity’s returns.

      Loss allowances for expected credit losses                      預期信貸虧損的虧損撥備

      risks of a default occurring as the weights.

      measures the loss allowance for a financial instrument          初始確認以來已大幅增加，本集團將按相
      at an amount equal to the expected credit losses                等於該金融工具的預計存續期內所有可能
      that result from all possible default events over the           發生的違約事件導致的預期信貸虧損（「存
      expected life of that financial instrument (“lifetime           續期預期信貸虧損」）的金額就貿易應收款
      expected credit losses”) for trade receivables and              項及合同資產計量一項金融工具的虧損撥
      contract assets, or if the credit risk on that financial        備。
      instrument has increased significantly since initial

      on a financial instrument (other than trade receivables         合同資產除外）的信貸風險自初步確認後
      and contract assets) has not increased significantly            並未大幅增加，則本集團將按相等於存續
      since initial recognition, the Group measures the loss          期預期信貸虧損部份（指報告期後12個月
      allowance for that financial instrument at an amount            內可能發生的違約事件所導致的該金融工
      equal to the portion of lifetime expected credit losses         具的預期信貸虧損）的金額計量該金融工
      that represents the expected credit losses that result          具的虧損撥備。

     over time or at a point in time. A performance
     obligation is satisfied over time if:

     (a)   the customer simultaneously receives and                 (a)   當客戶同時取得及消費本集團履約
           consumes the benefits provided by the Group’s                  所提供的利益；
           performance;

     (b)   the Group’s performance creates or enhances an           (b)   當本集團的履約行為創建或改良一
           asset that the customer controls as the asset is               項其於被創建或改良時受客戶控制

           for performance completed to date.                             強制執行付款的權利。

     revenue is recognised by reference to the progress             已 完 成 履 約 責 任 的 進 度 確 認。否 則，收 益

     obligation. Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point

      Taxation                                                          稅項

      Income tax represents the sum of the current tax and              所得稅指即期稅項及遞延稅項的總和。
      deferred tax.

      The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for          即期應付稅項按年內應課稅溢利計算。應
      the year. Taxable profit differs from profit recognised in        課稅溢利與於損益內確認的溢利不同，原
      profit or loss because it excludes items of income or             因是應課稅溢利不包括其他年度的應課稅
      expense that are taxable or deductible in other years             或可予扣稅的收入或開支項目，亦不包括
      and it further excludes items that are never taxable              免稅或不可扣稅的項目。本集團的即期稅
      or deductible. The Group’s liability for current tax is           項負債按報告期末前已頒佈或實際頒佈的
      calculated using tax rates that have been enacted                 稅率計算。
      or substantively enacted by the end of the reporting
      period.

      Deferred tax is recognised on differences between                 遞延稅項按財務報表內資產及負債賬面值
      the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the             與計算應課稅溢利所用相關稅基的差額確
      financial statements and the corresponding tax bases              認。遞延稅項負債一般按所有應課稅暫時
      used in the computation of taxable profit. Deferred               差額確認入賬，而遞延稅項資產則會在可
      tax liabilities are generally recognised for all taxable          能有應課稅溢利可供作抵銷可予扣減暫時
      temporary differences and deferred tax assets are                 差額、未動用稅項虧損或未動用稅項抵免
      recognised to the extent that it is probable that taxable         時確認入賬。倘暫時差額因商譽或在不影
      profits will be available against which deductible                響應課稅溢利及會計溢利的交易中初次確
      temporary differences, unused tax losses or unused                認（業 務 合 併 除 外）其 他 資 產 及 負 債 而 產
      tax credits can be utilised. Such assets and liabilities          生，則有關資產及負債不予確認。
      are not recognised if the temporary difference arises
      from goodwill or from the initial recognition (other
      than in a business combination) of other assets and
      liabilities in a transaction that affects neither the
      taxable profit nor the accounting profit.

      Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable               於附屬公司的投資產生的應課稅暫時差額
      temporary differences arising on investments in                   乃確認為遞延稅項負債，惟本集團可控制
      subsidiaries except where the Group is able to control            暫時差額的撥回及有關暫時差額可能不會
      the reversal of the temporary difference and it is                於可預見將來撥回則除外。
      probable that the temporary difference will not reverse
      in the foreseeable future.

      The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed            遞延稅項資產的賬面值於各報告期末進行
      at the end of each reporting period and reduced to                檢討，並予以扣減直至不再可能有足夠應
      the extent that it is no longer probable that sufficient          課稅溢利可供收回全部或部份資產為止。
      taxable profits will be available to allow all or part of
      the asset to be recovered.

     that have been enacted or substantively enacted                   於損益中確認，惟倘遞延稅項與於其他全
     by the end of the reporting period. Deferred tax is               面收益或直接於權益確認的項目相關，則

     reflects the tax consequences that would follow from              團預期於報告期末可收回或結算其資產及

     relate to income taxes levied by the same taxation                準結算其即期稅項資產及負債時，則遞延
     authority and the Group intends to settle its current tax         稅項資產及負債可互相對銷。
     assets and liabilities on a net basis.

      Impairment of assets                                             資產減值

      At the end of each reporting period, the Group reviews           本集團於各報告期末檢討其有形及無形資
      the carrying amounts of its tangible and intangible              產（遞延稅項資產、投資、存貨及應收款項
      assets other than deferred tax assets, investments,              除 外）的 賬 面 值，以 釐 定 有 否 跡 象 顯 示 該
      inventories and receivables, to determine whether                等資產出現減值虧損。倘存在任何該等跡
      there is any indication that those assets have suffered          象，則估計資產的可收回金額以釐定任何
      an impairment loss. If any such indication exists,               減值虧損程度。倘無法估計個別資產的可
      the recoverable amount of the asset is estimated in              收回金額，則本集團估計資產所屬現金產
      order to determine the extent of any impairment loss.            生單位的可收回金額。
      Where it is not possible to estimate the recoverable
      amount of an individual asset, the Group estimates
      the recoverable amount of the cash-generating unit to
      which the asset belongs.

      Recoverable amount is the higher of fair value less              可收回金額為公平值減出售成本與使用價
      costs of disposal and value in use. In assessing value           值 兩 者 中 的 較 高 者。評 估 使 用 價 值 時，估
      in use, the estimated future cash flows are discounted           計未來現金流量會按照可反映現時市場對
      to their present value using a pre-tax discount rate that        貨幣時間值及資產特定風險的評估的稅前
      reflects current market assessments of the time value            貼現率，貼現至其現值。
      of money and the risks specific to the asset.

      If the recoverable amount of an asset or cash-                   倘資產或現金產生單位的可收回金額估計
      generating unit is estimated to be less than its carrying        低於其賬面值，則資產或現金產生單位的

      generating unit is reduced to its recoverable amount.            於損益確認，惟倘相關資產以重估價值列
      An impairment loss is recognised immediately in                  賬，在此情況下減值虧損視作重估價值減
      profit or loss, unless the relevant asset is carried at a        少處理。
      revalued amount, in which case the impairment loss is
      treated as a revaluation decrease.

      Where an impairment loss subsequently reverses, the              倘減值虧損於日後撥回，則資產或現金產
      carrying amount of the asset or cash-generating unit             生單位的賬面值增至其可收回金額經修
      is increased to the revised estimate of its recoverable          訂估計，惟增加後的賬面值不得高於假設
      amount, but so that the increased carrying amount                過往年度並無就資產或現金產生單位確認
      does not exceed the carrying amount that would have              減值虧損而釐定的賬面值（扣除攤銷或折
      been determined (net of amortisation or depreciation)            舊）。減值虧損撥回即時於損益確認，惟倘

      or cash-generating unit in prior years. A reversal of an         值虧損撥回視作重估價值增加處理。
      impairment loss is recognised immediately in profit or

3.   MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICY                                   3.   主要會計政策資料（續）
     INFORMATION (Continued)

     Provisions and contingent liabilities                             撥備及或然負債

     Provisions are recognised for liabilities of uncertain            當本集團因已發生的事件須承擔現有法定
     timing or amount when the Group has a present legal               或推定責任，而履行責任有可能導致經濟
     or constructive obligation arising as a result of a past          利益流出，並能可靠估計責任金額的情況
     event, it is probable that an outflow of economic                 下，須對該等時間或金額不確定的負債確
     benefits will be required to settle the obligation and a          認 撥 備。倘 貨 幣 時 間 值 重 大，則 撥 備 的 金
     reliable estimate can be made. Where the time value               額乃按預期用於履行該責任的支出的現值
     of money is material, provisions are stated at the                列賬。
     present value of the expenditures expected to settle
     the obligation.

     Where it is not probable that an outflow of economic              倘需要流出經濟利益的機會不大，或責任
     benefits will be required, or the amount cannot be                金額無法可靠估計，則責任乃披露為或然
     estimated reliably, the obligation is disclosed as a              負債，除非經濟利益流出的可能性極低則
     contingent liability, unless the probability of outflow           另 作 別 論。可 能 出 現 的 責 任（即 是 否 存 在
     is remote. Possible obligations, whose existence                  將僅取決於日後是否會發生一宗或多宗事
     will only be confirmed by the occurrence or non-                  件）亦披露為或然負債，惟經濟利益流出的
     occurrence of one or more future events are also                  可能性極低者則除外。
     disclosed as contingent liabilities unless the probability
     of outflow is remote.

     Events after the reporting period                                 報告期後事項

     Events after the reporting period that provide additional         為本集團於報告期末狀況提供額外資料或
     information about the Group’s position at the end of              顯示持續經營假設並不適合的報告期後事
     the reporting period or those that indicate the going             項均屬於調整事項，於綜合財務報表內反
     concern assumption is not appropriate are adjusting               映。並非屬調整事項的報告期後事項如屬
     events and are reflected in the consolidated financial            重大，則於綜合財務報表附註中披露。
     statements. Events after the reporting period that are
     not adjusting events are disclosed in the notes to the
     consolidated financial statements when material.

     The Group’s activities expose it to a variety of financial           本集團業務活動承受多項財務風險：外幣
     risks: foreign currency risk, credit risk, liquidity risk and        風 險、信 貸 風 險、流 動 資 金 風 險 及 利 率 風
     interest rate risk. The Group’s overall risk management              險。本集團的整體風險管理項目專注於金
     programme focuses on the unpredictability of financial               融市場的不可預測性及尋求降低對本集團
     markets and seeks to minimise potential adverse                      財務表現造成的潛在不利影響。
     effects on the Group’s financial performance.

      The fair value of the Group’s investment properties at           本集團於二零二�年十二月三十一日的投
      31 December 2025 is approximately RMB39,604,000                  資物業公平值約為人民幣39,604,000元（二
      (2024:RMB40,352,000). The fair value has been                    零二四年：人民幣40,352,000元）。公平值

      management.

      The fair value was determined based on the market                公平值乃根據市場可比方法釐定，其中評
      comparable approach, where the price per square                  估了所有物業單位的每平方米價格。每平
      metre of all units of the properties are assessed.               方米的市場價格參考該物業的待售單位以
      The market price per square metre are assessed by                及附近其他類似物業的價格予以評估。
      reference to the price achieved in the for-sale units of
      the properties as well as other similar properties in the
      neighbourhood.

      highest and best use of the properties is their current          佳用途乃其當前用途。
      use.

 25. TRADE AND BILLS PAYABLES                                    25. 貿易應付款項及應付票據

                                                                                 2025             2024
                                                                             二零二五年            二零二四年

                                                                             人民幣千元            人民幣千元
       Trade payables                             貿易應付款項                         218,754         184,900
       Bills payables                             應付票據                            16,024          18,293

                                                                                 234,778         203,193


      Bills payables were secured by the Group’s pledged bank      應付票據由本集團的質押銀行存款人民
      deposit of RMB16,024,000 (2024: RMB20,000,000) and           幣 16 , 0 2 4 , 0 0 0 元（二 零 二 四 年 ： 人 民 幣
      bills receivables of RMBnil (2024: RMB14,101,000).           20,000,000元）及應收票據人民幣零元（二
                                                                   零二四年：人民幣14,101,000元）擔保。

      The Group participates in a supply chain financing           本集團參與一項供應鏈融資安排(SCF)。根
      arrangement (SCF). Under the arrangement, a bank             據該安排，銀行同意就本集團所欠參與供
      agrees to pay amounts to a participating supplier in         應商的發票金額向其付款，並於日後從本

    Capital management                                                      資本管理

    The Group’s primary objective when managing capital                     本集團管理資本主要旨在保障本集團的持
    is to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a                    續經營能力，透過定價與風險水平相稱的
    going concern, so that it can continue to provide                       產 品 及 服 務，以 及 按 合 理 成 本 取 得 融 資，
    returns for shareholders and benefits for other                         從而能夠繼續為股東提供回報並為其他權
    stakeholders, by pricing products and ser vices                         益持有人帶來利益。本集團管理資本的目
    commensurately with the level of risk and by securing                   標為保障本集團持續經營的能力及通過優
    access to finance at a reasonable cost. The Group’s                     化債務及權益平衡使股東回報最大化。
    objectives when managing capital are to safeguard the
    Group’s ability to continue as a going concern and to
    maximise the return to the shareholders through the
    optimisation of the debt and equity balance.

    The Group manages its capital structure and makes                       本集團根據經濟狀況變動管理及調整資本
    adjustments to it, in light of changes in economic                      結 構。為 維 持 或 調 整 資 本 結 構，本 集 團 或
    conditions. To maintain or adjust the capital structure,                會調整股息派付、發行新股份、購回股份、
    the Group may adjust the payment of dividends, issue                    增加新債務、贖回現有債務或出售資產以
    new shares, buyback shares, raise new debts, redeem                     減少債務。
    existing debts or sell assets to reduce debts.
